- 1 - 
訪談速報 
巧新(1563) 
再生鋁與半導體雙引擎推升獲利 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $80 
前次投資建議：2026.05.07 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價52

報告日期：2026.8.11 研究員：陳泂暉

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/8/10 收盤價： 56.5 再生鋁採用率提升以及半導體產品放量，給予投資評等「買
進」 
目前股價 13X 27(F)PER，考量：

(1) 公司 26Q2 營收 19.8 億元，QoQ+5.8%、YoY+15.5%，
營收較上季增加主要是因為下游客戶備貨較為積極；毛利
率 24.3%，QoQ+2.3ppts、YoY+3.5ppts，因再生鋁使用
比率顯著提升與產品優化；稅後淨利 7,119.7 萬元，
QoQ-45.5%、較去年同期虧轉盈，因受到匯率衝擊以及營
業費用受到運輸成本上升影響；稅後 EPS 0.33 元。 
(2) 受新車旺季與高端車款拉貨帶動，預估輪圈出貨 2026 年
逾 60 萬顆（下半年優於上半年），2027 年達 70 萬顆。
公司目標營業利益率回升至 20%；再生鋁採用率達 80%，
經 5個月生產與海運發酵後，將顯著拉升毛利率。 
(3) 屏東二廠預計 27Q2 投產，帶動總熔煉產能達10萬噸 （1/3
自用、2/3外銷代工） 。半導體原生鋁預計 26Q3完成認證，
下半年啟動小量出貨並於 2027 年放量。 
(4) 預估 2027 年營收 85.12 億元 ，YoY+7.7%； 毛利率29.1%，
YoY+4ppts；稅後淨利9.82 億元，YoY+46.7%；稅後EPS 
4.35 元。

巧新目前股價位於 13X27(F)PER，考量到再生鋁使用比重提
高以及半導體產品放量，因此投資評等上調至「買進」，6個
月目標價 80(18.5X2027(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 41.6%。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐國際預估

訪談重點

公司為國內汽車鍛造輪圈大廠 
巧新成立於 1994 年，為國內汽車鍛造輪圈大廠，主要產品包含鍛造鋁圈、
鋁材、其他(主要為鍛造鋁懸吊系統元件 )。公司目前有三座生產基地，分別
是雲林場、屏東廠及德國廠，鍛造輪圈總年產能為90萬件(雲林廠 60 萬件、
屏東廠 30萬件)。

公司客戶包括全球知名車廠，Ferrari(法拉利)、Porsche(保時捷)、Mclaren(麥
拉倫)、Bently(賓利)、Aston Martin、賓士(含 AMG)、BMW(含 Rolls-Royce、
Mini)、Jaguar & Land Rover、Lexus、Lotus及電動卡車製造商 Workhouse
等。

客戶拉貨強勁抵銷匯損衝擊 
公司 26Q2 營收 19.8 億元，QoQ+5.8%、YoY+15.5%，營收較上季增加主
要是因為 下游客戶備貨較為積極 ；毛利率 24.3%， QoQ+2.3ppts、
YoY+3.5ppts，因再生鋁使用比率顯著提升與產品優化 ；稅後淨利 7,119.7
萬元，QoQ-45.5%、較去年同期虧轉盈，因受到匯率衝擊以及營業費用受到
運輸成本上升影響；稅後 EPS 0.33元。

半年輪圈出貨優於上半年，再生鋁採用率提升帶動毛利回升 
展望 26H2，鍛造輪圈受惠於車廠新車與改款車型通常於 10月開始組裝之旺
季需求，因此預計全年輪圈出貨量將超過 60 萬顆，且出貨下半年會優於上
半年。而 2027 年則是持續受惠於高端既有車款的熱門，帶動長期的需求以
及新車與改款的車型帶動下，預估 2027 年全年輪圈出貨量將達到 70萬顆

在毛利與成本表現方面，公司目標將整體營業利益率回升至 20%。由於產品
從場內生產製造（約 2個月）到海運運輸（約 3個月）至客戶端提貨認列存
在約 5 個月的時間差，故成本與毛利優化效益具延後發酵特性。公司目前獲
利優化之核心動能將來自再生鋁材的導入 ，由於再生鋁成本較原生鋁低約
10%至 15%，且截至 5、6月再生鋁佔鍛造輪圈原料採用率來到 80%，預計
將能有效提升毛利率。

- 3 - 
訪談速報 
半導體驗證與產能擴張雙軌併進，帶動長期結構獲利改善 
公司持續拓展鋁材多元應用，目前於屏東設立的首座水平熔煉廠（產能 4 萬
噸）運作穩定，第二座垂直熔煉廠正在建置中，預計 26Q3 完成裝機與參數
調整後，並於 27Q2 投產，總熔煉產能規模規劃將達 10萬噸（約 1/3 自用、
2/3 外銷或代工）。在半導體材料領域，公司持續推進設備腔體與零件之送
樣與驗證作業，原生鋁材料預計 26Q3 完成認證並於下半年啟動少量出貨，
並於 2027 年放量。

再生鋁採用率提升以及半導體產品放量，帶動營收獲利雙增 
預估 26Q3 營收 20.15 億元，QoQ+1.8%、YoY+18.4%；毛利率 25.6%，
QoQ+1.3ppts、YoY+9ppts，受惠於再生鋁採用率上升；稅後淨利 2.39 億元，
QoQ+235.8%、YoY+458.5%；稅後 EPS 1.06 元。

預估 2026年營收 79.02億元，YoY+13.3%；毛利率 25.1%，YoY+3.8ppts；
稅後淨利 6.7億元，YoY+110.4%；稅後 EPS 2.97元。

預估 2027年營收 85.12 億元，YoY+7.7%；毛利率 29.1%，YoY+4ppts；稅
後淨利 9.82 億元，YoY+46.7%；稅後 EPS 4.35 元。

巧新目前股價位於 13X27(F)PER，考量到再生鋁使用比重提高以及半導體產
品放量，因此投資評等上調至 「買進」，6個月目標價 80(18.5X2027(F)PER)，
潛在上漲空間 41.6%。

台玻 (1802 TT)  Buy

2Q26 獲利、 07/2026 營收均優於預期。  
投資建議： 台玻 2Q26 獲利及 07/2026 營收均優於預期，且受惠
於台系 CCL 大廠佈建 M9 材料，預估高階玻纖布營收比重持續
成長， 4Q26 玻纖布貢獻可望超越 50%。2026 年玻纖布年產能可
望較 2025 年成長 50%，加上積極擴大資本支出，增加 E 布及 T
布產能， 2027 年高階玻纖布營收貢獻將維持上行格局。故預估
台玻 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 2.14/3.94 元。給予投資建議
Buy ，目標價 63 元 (2026 年 PBR3.31X) 。  
富喬、建滔積層板調漲產品報價，顯示上游材料供需緊張： 富
喬於 06/26/2026 調漲 E 布報價 30% ，且大陸 CCL 龍頭建滔積
層板亦於 07/06/2026 再度分別調漲 FR-4 、 CEM-1/22F 及 PP 等
產品報價約 15%、10%、15%，顯示上游材料尚處供需緊張階段。  
2Q26 獲利達成率 128.1% ，優於預期： 台玻 2Q26 營收 120.62
億元， QoQ+17.74% 、YoY+16.84% 。伴隨高階玻纖布出貨提升，
帶動 2Q26 毛利率 QoQ+4.10% 至 23.33% ， 優於原預期之 21.53% 。
稅後純益 12.66 億元， QoQ+113.29% 、 YoY 虧轉盈，優於預期
之 9.88 億元。獲利達成率 128.1% 。稅後 EPS 0.44 元，亦優於
原預期之 0.34 元。  
07/2026 營收 MoM+8.90% ，優於預期： 台玻 07/2026 營收 45.81
億元， MoM+8.90% 、YoY+28.45% ，略優於預期，與原預期值差
異約 +5.4% 。主因為 Low Dk 高階玻纖布出貨動能較原預期佳。
以玻纖布占整體營收比重約 40~45% 、高階玻纖布約佔玻纖布
40~42% ，推估高階玻纖布營收 MoM+10~12% 。  
上修台玻 2026 年稅後 EPS 至 2.14 元： 鑒於玻纖布需求大幅提
升，預估 4Q26 底玻纖布營收比重可望達 52% 。 2Q26 毛利率優
於預期，故研究部調整 3Q26 毛利率至 25.47% ，並同步上修預
估台玻 2026 年營收 499.04 億元 ，YoY+20.27% ，毛 利 率24.92% ，
稅後純益 62.30 億元， YoY 虧轉盈，稅後 EPS 2.14 元。

2 群益投顧 
  台玻 ESG： 
ESG 方面，台玻堅守四大主軸，包括強化永續環境、落實誠信經營、關懷社
會公益及發展友善職場。強化永續環境方面，台玻在符合法規條件下，積極
減降生產過程對環境的外部成本，並妥善安置生產後產生之廢棄物；落實誠
信經營方面，台玻始終秉持該核心經營理念，與對股東、員工及利害關係人
之承諾及責任；關懷社會公益方面，透過參與社會公益活動，落實回饋社會
及關懷責任，並提供員工及經銷商子女相關獎學金，培育人才；發展友善職
場方面，尊重人權及性別平等，提供員工舒適且安全職場環境。  
公司簡介： 
台玻成立於 1964 年，事業群分別平板事業部、纖維事業部、容器事業部、
汽車玻璃事業部。主要產品包括平板玻璃、玻璃纖維及玻璃器皿，生產基地
位於台灣及中國兩地。平板玻璃產品均已通過歐盟 CE、美國 ANSI 、台灣
CNS 相關規範，低輻射 Low-E 複層玻能維持可見光情況下，阻擋紅外線熱
能進入室內，具良好隔熱效果，主要用於建築物玻璃帷幕牆。公司也有發展
玻璃基板，有望搭上 CoPoS 題材。玻璃纖維方面，台玻有複合材料用的 FRP
及玻璃纖維布。 FRP 玻璃纖維具備優異耐腐蝕耐候性、低成本及高強度特
性。玻璃纖維布廣泛運用於 5G 伺服器、 AI 伺服器、手機平板及筆電等產
品。3Q25 產品組合中，平板玻璃佔 60%、玻璃纖維佔 32%、玻璃器皿佔 8%。
圖一、台玻產品組合 (營收比重 )

資料來源：台玻，群益投顧預估彙整  
台玻訂單能見度達 2027 年，預估高階玻纖布市佔上看 45%： 
台玻從 1990 年開始生產玻纖布，技術並非一蹴可磯，而是多年人才培育及
技術經驗累積推敲而得。最開始是與美國 Owens Corning 簽署技術合作授
權，從中取得全球最先進玻璃纖維漿料配方及技術指導，並向日本 Kanebo
購入織布技術。台玻亦為繼 Nittobo 、 Ibiden 後，第三家廠商具備自行研發
量產 Low Dk 玻璃纖維布廠商，並已通過國內外大廠認證，於 2025 年陸續
出貨。主要應用於 5G 基礎建設、高階伺服器、高階顯卡及車用等領域。  
AI 投資強勁推升載板加速升級， Low Dk1 、 Low Dk2( 低介電 )玻纖布及 Low

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 11 日

3 群益投顧 
CTE( 低熱膨脹係數 )玻纖布需求隨之暴漲。現階段台玻產能已達滿載，且訂
單能見度至 2027 年。公司表示， 2026 年目標高階玻纖布市占率 40%~45% 。
富喬、建滔積層板調漲產品報價，顯示上游材料供需緊張： 
台玻於 2023 年陸續完成 Low Dk 、 Low CTE 玻纖布開發。惟 T-glass 尚待日
本大廠 Nittobo 於 2H27 後開出新產能，才得以部分緩解，且考量技術門檻
偏高、窯爐建置期長，產能放量亦需時間等待。目前台玻 T-glass 已通過國
內外廠商認證， Ibiden 亦表示 T-glass 仍處於供不應求階段。  
產品報價方面，富喬於 06/26/2026 調漲 E 布報價 30%，且大陸 CCL 龍頭建
滔積層板 04/28/2026 發布漲價通知，宣布即日此 FR-4 銅箔基板、半固化片
（ PP）將再調漲 10% 。 05/27/2026 再調漲板料報價 10% 、 PP 調漲 20% ，
07/06/2026 亦分別再次調漲 FR-4 、 CEM-1/22F 及 PP 等產品報價約 15% 、
10%、 15%，顯示上游材料尚處供需緊張階段。有別於中國以生產 E 布為主
力，台玻為搶占 AI 伺服器升級帶來之載板層數與面積同步擴大商機，積極
投入 T 布生產。若未來台玻市占率逐步提升，反映其市場競爭力，加上 4Q26
台系 CCL 大廠加速拉貨 E 布產品，台玻仍有望受惠漲價。  
2Q26 獲利達成率 128.1%，優於預期： 
台玻公布 2Q26 財報： 2Q26 營收 120.62 億元， QoQ+17.74% 、YoY+16.84% 。
伴隨高階玻纖布出貨提升，帶動 2Q26 毛利率 QoQ+4.10% 至 23.33% ，優於
原預期之 21.53% ，營業毛利 28.14 億元，營業費用 12.89 億元則略高於原預
期之 11.83 億元，加上所得稅 63 百萬元低於預期之 1.43 億元，帶動稅後純
益 12.66 億元， QoQ+113.29% 、 YoY 虧轉盈，優於預期之 9.88 億元。獲利
達成率 128.1% 。稅後 EPS 0.44 元，亦優於原預期之 0.34 元，差異約 +22.7% 。
07/2026 營收 MoM+8.90%，優於預期： 
台玻 07/2026 營收 45.81 億元， MoM+8.90% 、 YoY+28.45% ； 01~07/2026 累
計營收 268.81 億元， YoY+14.22% 。 07/2026 營收略優於預期，與原預期值
43.33 億元，差異約 +5.4% ，主因為 Low Dk 高階玻纖布出貨動能較原預期
佳。以玻纖布占整體營收比重約 40~45% 、高階玻纖布約佔玻纖布 40~42% ，
推估高階玻纖布營收貢獻約 7.3~8.7 億元， MoM+10~12% 。研判因玻璃纖維
營收貢獻成長，使得平板玻璃及玻璃器皿之合計營收貢獻約呈持平至略減。
上修台玻 2026 年稅後 EPS至 2.14 元： 
為因應市場需求暴漲及石英布 (Q 布 )成本較 Low Dk2 高出 4 倍，預估 2026
年持續開出新產能，高階玻纖布年產能由 2025 年約 2,000 萬米，
YoY+40~50% 至 2,800~3,000 萬米，主要用於擴充 Low Dk2 產能。  
05/11/2026 經董事會宣布，為因應市場對於高階玻纖布需求上揚，新增資本
支出 22.42 億元，用以擴建公司桃園廠及鹿港廠窯爐及冷修案，預計主要用
於增加 Low Dk2 、 T-glass 產能、提高市佔率，最快 2Q27 投產。 06/12/2026
股東會董事長指出， 2026 年預計再增加 20 億元資本支出，用於擴充 Low 
Dk( 低介電損耗 )、 Low CTE( 低熱膨脹係數 )之 T 布產能，並表示產品供不應
求將延續至 2027 年底 。 目前高階玻纖布擴產計畫共分三期 ， 第一期已於2026
年投入並量產，第二期預計 2026 年完成，新增之 20 億元為第三期投資計
畫，預計 2027 年完工。

個股報告

4 群益投顧 
2027 年因 Nvidia AI 伺服器 CCL 材料採用 M9，並搭配運用 Q 布，且目前
台玻也有 Q 布的技術儲備，未來若良率不錯，亦有望送樣驗證，具想像性。
鑒於玻纖布需求大幅提升、平板玻璃產量大致持平，以及毛利較低之玻璃器
皿比重減降下，預估 4Q26 底玻纖布營收比重可望達 52%，超越平板玻璃及
玻璃器皿合計 48% 。 2Q26 毛利率達 23.33% 優於原預期之 21.53% ，差異約
+1.80% ，故研究部調整 3Q26 毛利率至 25.47% ，稅後 EPS 預估為 0.63 元，
並同步上修預估台玻 2026 年營收 499.04 億元 ，YoY+20.27% ， 毛利率24.92% ，
稅後純益 62.30 億元， YoY 虧轉盈，稅後 EPS 2.14 元。

投資建議： 
台玻 2Q26 獲利及 07/2026 營收均優於預期，且受惠於台系 CCL 大廠佈建
M9 材料，預估高階玻纖布營收比重持續成長， 4Q26 玻纖布貢獻可望超越
50% 。 2026 年玻纖布年產能可望較 2025 年成長 50% ，加上積極擴大資本支
出，增加 E 布及 T 布產能， 2027 年高階玻纖布營收貢獻將維持上行格局。
故預估台玻 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 2.14/3.94 元。給予投資建議 Buy，
目標價 63 元 (2026 年 PBR3.31X) 。

無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Applied Materials, Inc. (AMAT-US)  
財報 FIRST TAKE — AI 產能大基建的最大贏家，財測超標但「不夠驚喜」

 評論及分析 (Overall Summary) 
Applied Materials, Inc. (AMAT) 2026 財年第三季 (Q3 FY2026) 財報展現公司
受惠於全球 AI 基礎設施的快速建設與對先進晶片需求激增的強勁動能。憑藉
在 AI 運算相關高效能與高價值技術領域的領導地位，公司實現創紀錄的單季
營收與獲利，並創下歷史最高的季增長率。在半導體系統與全球服務業務  
(AGS) 雙引擎發力推動下，單季營收達到 91 億美元。儘管面臨建廠無塵室空
間受限與短期擴產的人力成本挑戰，但客戶為確保供應鏈穩定，積極提供長
達八個季度的滾動預測，針對技術藍圖的能見度甚至延伸至 2030 年。隨著業
界迅速朝向先進邏輯製程、 DRAM 及先進封裝  (Advanced Packaging) 發
展，Applied Materials 透過其深厚的材料工程技術與 EPIC 創新平台，已進一
步鞏固其市場領導地位。展望 2026 財年第四季，在 DRAM 強勁復甦與領先
晶圓代工邏輯製程推動下，公司預期營收將再次大幅跳升，持續作為 AI 發展
趨勢下的主要受惠者。 
公司財報重點數據： 
Applied Materials 在 2026 財年第三季展現強勁的財務表現，單季總營收達 
91 億美元，不僅季增 15%，年增更達 25%，創下歷史新高。受惠於價值定價 
(Value-based pric ing) 策略及高利潤產品組合， Non-GAAP 毛利率微升至  
50.4%，較上季成長  40 個基點，較去年同期增加  150 個基點；同時  Non-
GAAP 營業利益率也擴張至 34% 的歷史新高，年增 330 個基點。亮眼的營運
表現帶動第三季 Non-GAAP 每股盈餘 (EPS) 達 3.50 美元（+41%YoY）。 
公司業績表現： 
⚫ 半導體系統 (Semiconductor Systems) 業務：營收達創紀錄的 70 億美元 
(+27%YoY)。其中，DRAM 營收 (包含 HBM 封裝) 較去年同期增長 52% 
創下新高。在沉積 (包含 PVD、CVD 及磊晶)、材料改質、材料移除 (蝕刻
與 CMP) 以及製程診斷與控制 (PDC) 等多個領域皆創下營收紀錄。該部門 
Non-GAAP 毛利率升至 55.4%，Non-GAAP 營業利益達 27 億美元。 
⚫ 全球服務 (Applied Global Services, AGS) 業務：營收達 18 億美元 (年增 
22%)。受惠於訂閱服務成長與高交易性零件需求，並利用  AI 與倉儲自動
化提升營運效率，部門毛利率達 35.6%，營業利益率達 30.1%。目前全球
已有超過 37,000 個機台連接至其專屬的 AIx 軟體平台。 
⚫ 其他業務 (Other)：營收約 2.94 億美元，主要為顯示器 (Display) 業務。

複委託報告 August 14, 2026

### Page 2

2
公司展望與產業前景：
⚫ 2026 財政年度第四季指引 ：預估營收將落在  102.5 億美元區間  (+
51%YoY)。半導體系統營收預計約 79 億美元 (+62%YoY)，AGS 營收約
18.4 億美元  (年增  22%)。Non-GAAP 毛利率預估約  50.4%，Non-
GAAP EPS 預計為 4.02 美元 (+85%YoY)。
⚫ 先進封裝與 PDC 前景：預期 2026 日曆年的整體先進封裝營收將成長超
過 70%，而製程診斷與控制 (PDC) 業務在今年也將成長超過 50%。
⚫ 產能擴張與中國市場 ：為了應對強勁的市場需求，公司本季新增超過
1,500 名製造與客服人員，並計畫在 2028 年前將系統產出能力翻倍。中
國市場本季佔半導體系統與 AGS 總營收的 26%，在 28 奈米代工邏輯投
資推動下，預期今年將持續成長。
電話會議 Q&A 重點摘要：
⚫ 訂單能見度與長期合約 ：管理層指出，針對先進晶片供不應求的情況，
大型客戶提供了長達八個季度的詳細滾動式預測，部分關鍵客戶針對技

術藍圖的能見度更延伸至  2030 年。這種高能見度顯著改善了公司與供
應鏈的產能規劃。
⚫ 毛利率與定價策略動態 ：分析師關注毛利率在營收大幅增長下於下一季
度的指引為何持平。管理層解釋，短期內因大量招募工程師與服務人員
產生了「爬坡成本」(Ramp costs) 抵銷了部分規模經濟優勢。然而，透
過「價值定價」(Value-based pricing) 策略，半導體系統毛利率已穩居
55% 以上，長期來看整體毛利率仍將保持增長趨勢。
⚫ ICAPS 與各終端市場復甦：針對 ICAPS ( 物聯網、通訊、汽車、電源與
感測器) 市場，公司預期該領域的設備消化期已接近尾聲，隨著客戶端
產能利用率上升以及電源、光子晶片的亮點需求，預期 ICAPS 整體在今
年及明年皆將重返成長軌道。
公司簡介：
Applied Materials, Inc. 是全球領先的半導體與顯示器設備及材料工程解決方
案供應商。該公司致力於推動資料中心、人工智慧  (AI) 運算基礎架構與先進
應用的底層晶片製造技術。其廣泛的產品線涵蓋沉積、材料移除、先進封裝

至製程診斷控制系統 (PDC)，並透過 Applied Global Services (AGS) 提供高
度依賴  AI 預 測 分 析 的 機 台 最 佳 化 解 決 方 案 。 近 期 更 透 過 設 立 EPIC
(Equipment and Process Innovation and Commercialization) 中心，進一
步深化與頂尖晶片製造商及研究機構的早期共同創新 (Co-innovation)。
競爭對手與公司自身價值：
Applied Materials 在全球半導體製造設備市場中面臨眾多同業的競爭。相較
於競爭對手， AMAT 的核心價值與護城河在於其最廣泛且高度互補的技術組
合 (特別在先進邏輯製程、DRAM 及先進封裝)。該公司透過全新的 EPIC 平台
讓客戶在架構發展的極早期階段便能接觸最新設備創新，從而加速新技術導
入並提升晶圓廠的生產良率與單位面積產出。這種深度的技術整合與協助客
戶提升廠區投資報酬率的能力，使其在 AI 軍備競賽中擁有無可取代的產業地

位。

廣達(2382) 
建議：維持買進 
結論 ：毛利率 、 營益率雖承壓 ， 但營運及獲利成長無虞，
且 PER 偏低

一、2Q26 毛利率 、 營益率優於預期 ， 本業達成率102%，
業外淨收入遠優於預期，獲利達成率 111%，單季稅後
EPS 7.11 元，累計 1H26 稅後 EPS 12.36 元 
廣達公告 2Q26 營收 1.036 兆元 、+28%QoQ、+106%YoY，
Server 及 NB 兩大產品線均強勁季成長雙位數，其中
Server 成長高於 NB，毛利率 5.02%(原 5%)、
+0.24pptsQoQ、-2.03pptsYoY，毛利率在 1Q26 新舊品
交替 ASP 變動顯著影響，2Q26 影響已和緩，Server 機
櫃 ASP 大致持平，但仍受到 Memory、CPU 及其他材
料成本上升影響，營業利益 342 億元、+48%QoQ、
+68%YoY，達成率 102%，略優於預期，其中費用率持
續創新低來到 1.72%、-0.22pptsQoQ，主要受惠營收規
模快速擴大帶來營運槓桿，營益率 3.3%(原 3.25%)、
+0.45pptsQoQ、-0.75pptsYoY， 業外淨收入23.17億元，
高於預期，其中匯兌收益高達 32.73 億元、利息淨支出
16.93 億元 ， 稅前淨利365 億元 、+49%QoQ、+69%YoY，
稅後淨利 286 億元、+35%QoQ、+70%YoY，獲利達成
率 111%， 單季稅後EPS 7.11 元(原 6.37 元)。 累計1H26
營收 1.845 兆元、+86%YoY，營業利益 572 億元、
+27%YoY，稅前淨利 610 億元、+30%YoY，稅後淨利
498 億元、+37%YoY，稅後 EPS 12.36 元。

二、Server 占營收比重逾 8 成 ， 其中AI Server 占 Server
比重約 75~80%，ASIC 比重仍低僅占 AI Server 約 10%；
2Q26 NB 季增雙位數，淡季不淡  
Server 占營收比重已穩定超過 80%，1H26 AI Server
占 Server 營收比重約 75~80%，目前前 AI Server 主要
營收來自 Top 5 CSP 客戶，隨著逐步擴展 NeoCloud 及
ASIC 客戶 ， 有利於改善客戶集中度並提高產品多元性，
NeoCloud GPU Server 及 CSP ASIC Server 專案均已
小量出貨，惟 ASIC 目前占 AI Server 比重仍<10%。 
2Q26 NB 營收占營收比重約低雙位數，出貨量約 1,150
萬台、+15%QoQ、-5%YoY，顯著優於公司原先預期的
持平或小幅成長，亦優於過去 2Q 季節性增幅，主要係
因品牌客戶預期 Memory 價格持續上漲，產能受到 AI 
Server 排擠，而提前備貨。雖如此，但 Memory、CPU
等零組件缺貨及價格持續上漲 ， 仍壓抑NB 毛利率表現。 
2Q26 智慧駕駛及電動車相關產品營收均呈雙位數季成
長及年成長，顯示車載產品組合調整大致已告一段落，
終端市場亦逐步回溫，因 AI Server 營收快速擴大，車
載業務營收占比被稀釋至低個位數，但仍持續取得新專
案，並規劃擴充德國智慧駕駛相關產線。 
三、因應未來AI 龐大需求，除持續擴增美國加州及泰國
廠外，今年底產能倍增，擬取得友達華亞廠因應 2028
年後的需求，資本支出由 300 億元上修至 400 億元 
2Q26 資本支出 136 億元，累計 1H26 資本支出 218 億
元，主要用以美國加州廠及泰國廠的廠房設備擴充，占
資本支出比重約 8 成 ， 公司持續進行AI 相關產能擴充，
2026 年年底達產能倍增的目標(相對 2025 年底)。 同時，
廣達擬以 197 億元取得友達光電的華亞廠，該廠預計
4Q27 完成點交 ， 用以支應2028 年的 Server 訂單需求，
該廠具備較高的電力容量配置，對高階 AI Server 快速
擴充產能具有關鍵優勢，該廠加入後，有利於 2028 年
台灣 AI Server 後段組裝產能倍增，亦因此廣達調整
2026 年資本支出由 300 億元上修至 400 億元。 
四、3Q26 成長動能仍來自 AI Server 與 General，NB
出貨則季哀退 2 成以上，惟毛利率仍承壓，力拼營益率
持穩 1H26 水準 ，推估營收季增 10%， 單季稅後EPS 7.54
元 
展望 3Q26，NB 需求展望保守，零組件成本上升迫使品
牌客戶調高 NB 售價，進一步抑制消費者需求，預期
3Q26 出貨量將季衰退逾 2 成；Server 中，AI Server 營
收持續維持強勁的雙位數季成長 ， 但因基期已明顯提高 ，
其成長幅度將低於 2Q26，General Server 亦維持強勁
成長，且成長率高於GPU Server，惟 GPU Server 單價

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報 
遠高於 General Server，因此 AI Server 仍是營收主要
成長來源，GPU Server 3Q 及 4Q 都持續成長，惟各客
戶新平台導入時點及專案交付排程不同，單季營收及毛
利率仍會波動，營益率方面，3Q26 雖有部分方案、客
戶採 Consignment 的商業模式取代 B&S， 但仍占少數，
比重偏低，毛利率仍會受產品組合影響，但營益率則有
機會止穩於 1H26 水準。投顧預估 3Q26 營收 1.14 兆元
(原 1.015 兆元)、+10%QoQ、+130%YoY，其中 7 月營
收 3,662 億元、-5%MoM、+131%YoY，季營收達成率
32%，累計營收+93%YoY。合併毛利率 5%、
-0.02pptsQoQ、-1.85pptsYoY，合併營益率 3.4%、
+0.1pptsQoQ、-0.31pptsYoY，稅後淨利 304 億元、
+6%QoQ、+85%YoY，稅後 EPS 7.54 元(原 6.26 元)。 
五、展望 2026 年，AI Server 維持三位數成長，通用
Server 維持雙位數成長，NB 出貨量雙位數衰退，上修
2026 年營收至 YoY 96%，稅後 EPS 27.79 元 
展望 2026 年，NB 出貨量維持先前與產業平均相當的看
法，記憶體短缺帶動成本與售價上揚，削弱使用者採購
意願，使市場能見度偏低，客戶規劃轉趨保守，2026 年
出貨量將有雙位數年衰退；Server(包括 General Server
及 AI Server)能見度很高，客戶已開始討論 2027~2028
年的產能、電力規劃及電力需求解決方案，公司高度樂
觀看待 2027 及 2028 年需求，部分客戶已提供延伸至
2028 年的需求規劃。其中 General Server 全年展望維
持雙位數成長目標，AI Server 仍是主要成長動能，維持
年成長三位數百分比 ，1H26 AI Server 占 Server 比重約
75~80%，2H26 比重仍將進一步提高，預期 2026 年 AI 
Server 占 Server 營收 80%的目標不變。整體Server 自
1Q26 起已穩定占合併營收的 80%以上 ，NB 已降低至低
雙位數營收比重，此趨勢仍將持續。營益率方面，公司
預期營益率目標能維持 1H26 3.1%的水準，2H26 AI 
Server 占比提升有助產品組合，但 NB 出貨下滑將不利
經濟規模，兩者間將相互抵銷。投顧預估 2026 年營收
4.168 兆元(原 3.915 兆元)、+96%YoY， 毛利率4.96%、
-2.02pptsYoY，營益率 3.28%、-0.84pptsYoY，稅後淨
利 1,120 億元、YoY49%，稅後 EPS 27.79 元(原 24.5
元)。同時預估 2027 年營收 5,77 兆元、+38%YoY，毛
利率 4.67%、-0.29pptsYoY， 營益率3.2%、-0.07pptsYoY，
稅後淨利 1,446 億元 、+29%YoY， 稅後EPS 35.87 元。 
六、維持買進理由 
投顧預估 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 27.79 元及
35.87 元，目前股價325 元，PER <12X 及 9X 不高，短
期毛利率雖仍承壓、但營益率已回穩，隨 VR 2H26 及
1H27 出貨，毛利率、營益率仍舊承壓，惟營益金額仍能
顯著年成長 ， 故PER 仍難回到過去榮景 ， 隨營收環境的
成長無虞，股價基期偏低下，仍有利走勢，故維持買進
投資評等，目標價維持 420 元(PER 15X 2026EPS)。

### Page 3

Taiwan PCB & Laminates
12 August 2026 Citi Research
3
Tripod: 2Q26 GM beat on enhanced product mix — Tripod’s 2Q26 sales (+19% QoQ)
were driven by the server segment (+40% QoQ), followed by auto (+13% QoQ) and
memory (+5% Qo). 2Q26 GM expanded 3.6ppt QoQ to 30.1%, beating
Citi/consensus forecasts by 1.8ppt/2.8ppt. Mgmt. attributed GM upbeat to
enhanced product mix and price hike to reflect inflation. OP levels also saw better
leverage QoQ, beating Citi/consensus forecasts by 14%/18%, while high tax rate was

more of a one-off. Overall earnings were still a beat by 4%/8% vs Citi/consensus
forecasts.
Tripod: 3Q26 outlook: server continuing picking up — As teir-1 PCB makers focus
more on leading AI server boards, Tripod sees more outflowing demand for either
general-purpose server mainboard or ASIC CPU mainboard. The company expects
its 3Q26 would be driven by continued server demand and price hike (especially for
auto customers). Thus, we now forecast Tripod’s 3Q26E sales to grow 13% QoQ. For

GM, we forecast 3Q26E GM of 31.2% (+1.1ppt QoQ). For memory demand, the
company now sees momentum gaining in 2H26 vs 1H26.
Tripod: CCL price hike not an issue — As per management’s observation, they don’t
think CCL price hike would become an issue for its GM profile, as customers in need
are fully aware of the CCL shortage and would accept PCB price hikes. Besides,
customers now would need to leverage PCB makers’ relationships with CCL
suppliers in order to secure more CCL supplies, favoring those PCB names with high-

tiering and large scale. For its CCL supply, mgmt. thinks its supply is tight now but
has not seen the shortage constraint its 3Q26 operation. For capacity expansion,
the company is ramping its Vietnam plant and is likely to see limited contribution in
2H26, and is expanding its capacity in Hubei plants.
Earnings revisions; GCE’s TP slightly cut to NT$1,630; Tripod’s TP lifted to
NT$700; Buy on both names — For GCE, we adjust our 2026/27 /28E earnings by -

1%/+2%/+3% to factor in short-term CCL price hike impact but lower tax rate. We
cut our GCE TP to NT$1,630 (25x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,740 (26x 2027E EPS). For
Tripod, we increase our 2026/27E/28E earnings by +10%/+4%/+4% to factor in its
stronger GM profile. We raise our DCF-based Tripod TP to NT$700 (implied 18x
2027E) from NT$666 (implied 17x 2027E EPS). We see both names benefitting from
favorable product mix and price hikes in the coming quarters. We rate both Tripod

and GCE Buy.

### Page 11

Taiwan PCB & Laminates
12 August 2026 Citi Research
11
Gold Circuit Electronics
Company description
Founded in 1981, Gold Circuit Electronics (GCE) is a Taiwanese company
specializing in the production of printed circuit board (PCB) including double-
sided PCB, multi-layer PCB, and HDI. GCE’s products primarily focus on
applications of servers, networking, laptops, base stations, mobile phones,
and tablets. The company is known for its high-layer count design

capabilities (maximum layer count: +50L) in the PCB industry, which help it to
penetrate into more high-end applications such as AI servers and 400G
networking. Currently, server/networking accounts for +70% of its total sales.
The company has four plants, with one located in Taiwan and the other three
in China, and plans to build one plant in Thailand.
Investment strategy
We rate GCE a Buy as we believe its high-tech capabilities in
server/networking design will allow it to catch more AI opportunities from US

CSPs or US IC designers in the AI boom cycle. We expect its rising sales
exposure toward high-end applications with better margins and improving
UTR to drive its margin going forward. In our view, geopolitical tensions
should benefit non-China suppliers more, which would prevent GCE from
fierce ASP competition from China PCB peers.
Valuation
Our TP of NT$1,630 for GCE is based on 25x our 2027E EPS estimate, which is
higher than 22x or +2std level of its 1-year-forward PE over the past three

years. We believe the 25x multiple is justified by GCE's high AI
server/networking sales exposure, market share gain in existing ASIC
customer and new AI ASIC customer wins from 4Q26, which drives its AI ASIC
sales growth and margin profile. Recall that TSMC expects close to a 50%+
CAGR in the next five years for its AI-related demand, which is stronger than
any other applications. Thus, we believe investors would be willing to pay for
high-growth AI names (especially ASIC) with high PE multiples. Our target

price is equivalent to 44x/25x our 2026/27E EPS estimates and 16.5x/9.9x
our 2026/27E BVPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected server recovery; 2) slower ramp-up
pace of new server platform; 3) less-than-expected AI demand; 4) more PCB
peers added into AI supply chain; and 5) stricter practice of China+1 by
customers due to geopolitical concerns.
Tripod Technology

Company description
Established in 1997, Tripod is a major printed circuit board (PCB) supplier,
ranked   No.9 globally   by Prismark in 2025. Tripod has manufacturing sites in
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 12

Taiwan PCB & Laminates
12 August 2026 Citi Research
12
Taiwan and Wuxi, China. The company has a diversified product portfolio
including DRAM, TFT-LCD, HDD, HDI, PC, server, and automotive.
Investment strategy
We rate Tripod a Buy. We like Tripod for its management quality, solid
execution, and product-mix improvement. We believe resilient growth from
memory, server/networking, and auto businesses will offset soft demand
from PCs/handsets and improve the overall margin profile at the same time.

Increasing contribution from HDI products should also help margins. With
prudent capex, stable depreciation level, and solid operations, we expect the
PCB business to generate cash flow to support a decent dividend yield. With
AI demand booming and potential supply tightness in high-end PCBs, we
believe Tripod could benefit.
Valuation
We use DCF methodology to derive our target price of NT$700. Key
assumptions of our DCF model include a risk-free rate of 4.5% (10-year

government bond rate), a market risk premium of 7%, and an equity beta of
0.8 (1-year weekly share price), which derives a WACC of 9.7%. Our model
assumes c.2% long-term revenue growth and gradually rising EBIT margin
from 23.6% in 2027E. Our target price is equivalent to 23x/18x our 2026/27E
EPS estimates and 5.8x/5.0x our 2026/27E BVPS estimates, which we
believe are more reflective of its current fundamentals.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent Tripod shares from reaching our target

price are: 1) weaker-than-expected tech demand; 2) increasing China labor
costs, rising gold/copper prices, and/or RMB appreciation, which would
pressure margins; 3) a sustained drop in conventional PCB pricing; 4) less HDI
sales mix; 5) less server/networking/memory sales exposure; and 6) less-
than-expected AI growth.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 6

Inventec (2356.TW)
11 August 2026 Citi Research
6
Inventec
Company description
Established in 1975 and listed in 1996, Taiwan-based Inventec is a key ODM
of NBs, servers, smartphones, and wearable/Io T devices to global customers.
Major shareholders include the founder Mr. Yeh Kuo-I, whose family has (both
directly and indirectly) a c35% stake.
Investment strategy
We rate Inventec shares as Buy. We expect stable demand in NB and server to
help support near-term earnings, while product diversification within smart

devices to areas such as Io T and medical space should help improve L-T
margins. We expect the company's solid cash yield to serve as a downside
cushion.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$75 for Inventec shares is based on 17x 2027 EPS, which
is slightly above the mid-point of its historical trading range, reflecting the
company's product diversification and stable demand outlook led by servers
and commercial NBs, as well as increasing AI server exposure that could

potentially drive margin expansion and better earnings momentum.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) Slower-than-expected shipment growth/weaker-than-expected
sell-through in HomePods and AirPods could lead to downside in our sales
forecasts; 2) If competitors adopt aggressive pricing strategies to gain market
shares, our ASP and margin assumptions could face downside risk; 3) Lower-
than-expected server margins driven by rising exposure to Chinese customers

could impact our earnings forecasts negatively; 4) Deterioration in NB
demand and investment in solar would hurt our forecasts; and 5) Stronger-
than-expected Taiwan dollar could lead to FX loss and downside to our
current forecasts.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Micro-Star
Valuation
Our target price of NT$118 is based on c.12x 2026E EPS, below the stock's five-year average forward 12-month PE. This
valuation reflects the earnings downside risks from muted PC demand and margin pressure from soaring component costs,
while accounting for the time required for the server business to achieve meaningful scale.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause MSI's shares to trade above our target price include: 1) a sharper-than-expected recovery in

high-end gaming demand; and 2) faster-than-anticipated revenue contribution from the AI server segment. Key downside
risks include: 1) a deeper-than-expected contraction in global PC shipments; 2) prolonged supply shortages for gaming GPUs
due to Ai resource crowding; and 3) inability to pass through surging memory and CPU costs to price-sensitive consumers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

## Document 081: 260811_daiwa_Taiwan Microloops

Source PDF: all report pdf/260811_daiwa_Taiwan Microloops.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-11
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Microloops released its 2Q26 results on 11 August after market hours. Overall, 
its 2Q26 operating profit of TWD112m (+100.9% QoQ, +1.9% YoY) was largely in line with 
our and the consensus estimates. Likely with some FX losses, its 2Q26 EPS came in at 
TWD1, missing our and the consensus estimates of TWD1.09.

We have a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD1,325, based on a target PER of 40x 
applied to our one-year-forward EPS forecast. For more information on the company, please 
refer to our latest report, Positive outlook over 2026-28, published on 28 July 2026.

What’s the impact 
• 2Q26 results: core business largely in line with estimates. Microloops’ 2Q26 
revenue of TWD1,047m (+22.5% QoQ, +0.6% YoY) was in line with our and the 
Bloomberg consensus estimates. Its 2Q26 gross margin came in at 21.5% (vs. 18.2% in 
1Q26 and 20.1% in 2Q25), slightly lower than our (22.0%) and the consensus (22.5%) 
estimates. Its operating margin of 10.7% (vs. 6.5% in 1Q26 and 10.6% in 2Q25), was 
also slightly lower than our (10.9%) and the consensus (11.3%) estimates. Thus, its 
operating profit of TWD112m (+100.9% QoQ, +1.9% YoY) was roughly in line with our 
and the consensus estimates. Likely with some FX losses, its net profit of TWD68m 
(+51.5% QoQ, +166.2% YoY) missed our and the consensus estimates by 8.5% and 
8.1%, respectively. Its 2Q26 EPS finished at TWD1, missing our and the consensus 
estimates of TWD1.09.

• Intact revenue outlook over 2026-28E - at least double YoY. Due to likely rising 
liquid-cooling penetration over 2026-28 in ASIC and NIC cards, management maintains 
its revenue guidance of at least doubling YoY over 2026-28. Besides, amid a rising level 
of automation and improving production procedures, management expects the 
company’s gross and operating margins to improve QoQ in 2H26. Management also 
expects the revenue momentum to pick up MoM in 2H26, translating into record-high 
monthly revenue from July to December 2026. In 4Q26, the revenue drivers will include 
ASICs and CPUs, in our view. Microloops aims for a new CSP client to start contributing 
revenue in 2Q27, with another new CSP client expected to come on board in 2028.

What we recommend 
We have a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD1,325, based on a target PER of 40x and 
applied to our 1-year forward EPS forecast. Based on our 2026/27E EPS, the stock is

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 1,500 TWD | Price: 964.00 TWD

Lighter GM/OPM in 2Q: higher material/depreciation costs 
Gold Circuit’s (GCE) 2Q26 OP was 3% above BofAe but 2% below consensus. GM/OPM 
of 34.7%/26.4% came in below BofAe of 35.8%/27.4% and consensus of 36.0%/28.1%. 
We believe the margin outcome reflected larger sales scale (+26% QoQ, +75% YoY; 
7%/5% above BofAe/consensus), still favorable mix (server/networking accounted for 
78%/15% of 2Q26 sales, vs. 72%/20% in 1Q26 and 74%/13% in 2Q25), and higher 
material/depreciation costs. NP was 13%/8% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3), thanks 
to a lower tax rate, despite a non-operating loss (details to be disclosed), 
Still intact sales/GM growth from 3Q but HDI likely for T4 
We now expect 3Q/4Q26 sales +28%/+1% QoQ (taking 2026E sales +77% YoY) and 
2027/28E sales +37%/+26% YoY, driven by new ASIC platform launches, ASP growth 
from server spec upgrades, and ongoing capacity expansion in Taiwan/Thailand/China. 
We also expect GM to increase QoQ from 3Q26 as higher material costs are passed 
through. Looking ahead to AWS Trainium 4 generation, we believe GCE will remain a key 
AWS supplier. However, our latest check suggests that HDI technology will likely be used 
for Trainium 4, vs. GCE’s current focus on multi-layer board products. We thus adopt 
more conservative GM assumptions to reflect 1) potentially higher upfront/production 
costs to make HDI boards and 2) higher depreciation costs from building more new sites.  
Lower PO to NT$1,500 but reiterate Buy 
We lift 2026-28E EPS by 6-9% mainly to reflect 2Q26 results, still solid sales/margin 
growth riding on the AI upcycle, and a lower tax rate (29% guidance vs. 32% earlier). We 
trim PO to NT$1,500 (25x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,810 (32x 2027E EPS), reflecting an 
unchanged valuation base but lower P/E multiple. We use 25x (2.5SD above P/E average 
since 2020, vs. prior 32x historical high) on slow margin expansion pace. Nevertheless, 
we continue to like GCE as a top ASIC server PCB maker amid strong AI demand, all-time 
focus on high-end AI server/networking PCBs, expanding margins. Reiterate Buy.

Company Sector 
Electronics

Company Description 
Gold Circuit (GCE) is a PCB company in Taiwan with high 
exposure in server (74% of 2025 sales) and networking 
(15%). Key end-clients are global CSPs and US top 
networking/telecom names. Besides its headquarters, the 
company has production sites in Taoyuan (Taiwan), Suzhou 
(China), Jiangsu (China), and Thailand, with ongoing capacity 
expansion. GCE was established in 1981 and listed on the 
Taiwan stock market in March 1998.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Gold Circuit, given (1) its high 
exposure and focus on better-margin server/networking 
business amid rising AI applications globally to keep high 
ASP/margin, (2) strong competitive advantages and high 
entry barrier as focusing on high-end products, (3) higher 
production flexibility after adding capacity in Thailand, (4) 
positive FCF/OCF since 2007/2019, respectively, with an 
improving balance sheet (from net debt to net cash) and 
expanding ROE.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
Gold Circuit (GOCCF) 
Our PO of NT$1,500 is based on 25x 2027E EPS. Our 25x valuation multiple (2.5SD 
above historical average since 2020 when 50%+ of sales from server) is similar to the 
average of its closest AI PCB peers' P/E multiple targets we set given (1) strong AI PCB 
market demand, (2) GCE's high AI server exposure, (3) solid sales mix from better-margin 
server/networking business, and (4) ongoing margin expansion to historical high level.

Upside risks: (1) stronger server/networking market demand to drive ASP/margins, (2) 
better utilization rate, (3) faster capacity expansion in Thailand, (4) lower raw 
material/labor/logistics costs, and (5) favorable FX movement.

Downside risks: (1) ASIC platform delay, (2) weaker market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (3) softer AI server demand due to AI chip short supply, (4) GM pressure 
from lower utilization rate, higher raw material/labor/logistics costs, and (5) unfavorable 
FX movement.

Key Takeaways
We underestimated Asustek's pricing power, and expect the stock to react

CY26 server revenue guidance raised to +150% y/y (up from +100% y/y).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lenovo (0992.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.3% (based
on a beta of 1.1, risk-free rate of 2.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Stronger PC demand recovery in China
n Better cost savings and improved product mix

n Lower-than-expected increase in memory price
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Weaker PC demand recovery in China
n Deterioration in cost savings and product mix
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory price
Acer Inc. (2353.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We use RI model for Acer's valuation. Key assumptions
include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 0.9, equity risk premium of 10.2% and risk-free rate

of 0.5%), a 6.0% medium-term growth rate, and a 3% terminal growth rate.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macro recovery than expected
n Share gains in the PC market
n Better-than-expected cost controls
n Lower-than-expected increase in memory cost
Risks to Downside
n Weaker macro recovery than expected
n Share loss in the PC market
n Worse-than-expected cost controls
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory cost
Asustek Computer Inc. (2357.TW)
Base case, sum of the parts:

We apply a 9x multiple to Asustek's 2026 brand profits. This is somewhat below the midpoint
of its historical band of 7-15x since 2009, which we view as fair because memory prices are
rising and will have a negative impact on margins.
We value its stakes in Pegatron and Advantech based on our price targets in proportion to
Asustek's holdings.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

### Page 1

SiPh capacity strong expansion through 2028E: Management targets capacity to 
grow at triple digit YoY growth in 2027/ 28E, riding on strong clients’ AI demand, 
rising SiPh penetration, also company’s capabilities to produce high-speed products. 
In mid June, Landmark announced the company has obtained equipment from 
Aixtron with total value at ~NT$837m, supporting its capacity expansion across InP 
Epi-Wafer and CW lasers. 
3-month preview: Landmark Jul revenues were up 177% YoY/ 19% MoM to 
NT$501m, or 14% higher than our previous estimates, reflecting strong demand on 
800G / 1.6T SiPh optical modules, better InP substrate supply, and Landmark’s 
capacity expansion. Looking ahead, we expect company’s Aug/ Sep revenues to 
grow at 173%/ 171% YoY to NT$506m/ NT$505m, supporting 3Q26E revenues to 
grow 174% YoY, or 24% QoQ to NT$1.5bn.

### Page 2

Earnings revision: We factor in Landmark 2Q26 result and revise up earnings by 4%/ 
1%/ 1%/ 1% in 2026-29E mainly on higher revenues of SiPh CW lasers, supported by 
strong client demand and capacity expansion. We revise up GM by 0.2ppts to 0.7ppts in 
2026-29E on improving product mix and larger scale.

Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology (unchanged) and apply a 61.5x 
(vs. previously at 61.0x) target P/E multiple on our 2029E EPS, discounted back to 
2027E, at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%, and market risk premium at 
5.1% - unchanged). Our target P/E multiple of 61.5x (vs. previously at 61.0x) is derived 
from the PEG&M ratio of peers (unchanged) in the photonics supply chain, reflecting an 
industry re-rating of product mix upgrade and rising demand on optical solution. 
Consequently, our 12-month target price is now NT$4,401 (vs. previously at NT$4,307). 
The implied 2028E P/E at 76x is between company’s avg. P/E at 56x and avg. +1 P/E at 
82x. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - LandMark Optoelectronics Corp.  
Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology, applying a 61.5x target P/E 
multiple on our 2029E EPS, then discount it back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, 
risk free rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12-month target 
price of NT$4,401. Our target P/E multiple is based on the same PEG&M ratio as peers in 
the photonics supply chain. We are Buy-rated on LandMark. 
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh penetration; stronger-than-expected competition; 
Integrated Device Manufacturers (IDMs) offering Epitaxial (EPI) wafers in-house.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 12 Aug 2026 close.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

2Q26 result overview 
Lotes’ 2Q26 core business (OPI) was 36%/29% lower than GSe 
and BBG consensus, with GM 5.2ppt/4.1ppt lower than GSe/BBG 
consensus, which we believe was driven by higher material costs 
while low-cost inventory was largely depleted in 1Q26. The OPEX to 
sales ratio was 3.8ppt/3.5ppt higher than GSe and BBG consensus, 
due to increasing investment in R&D for new products. For earnings, 
Lotes’ NI was 23%/17% lower than GSe/BBG consensus, mainly 
driven by the negative impact on GM from rising material costs. 
Furthermore, the company announced plans to issue up to 
NT$3.1bn of convertible bonds and up to 1.3mn of new shares 
(we estimate the share issuance could raise ~NT$1.8bn, assuming an 
issuance price equivalent to 70% (based on the discount % 
mentioned in the announcement) of today’s close price of 
NT$2,010, and would imply ~1.1% EPS dilution if fully issued). We 
believe the capital raise is likely to fund capacity expansion for 
future AI-related products, as the company previously mentioned 
AI-related contribution will expand to double-digit of total revenue 
over the next 3-5 years.  
Valuation 
We maintain our Neutral rating with a 12-m TP of NT$1,790, based 
on 17.5x 2H26-1H27E P/E, which is +0.5 SD above the company’s 
average valuation since 2020.  
Key risks: (1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected server shipment 
growth which would raise/lower server CPU socket demand; (2) 
lower-/higher-than-expected component costs without product 
pricing adjustment; (3) slower-/faster-than-expected AI 
server-related contribution

Bull/Bear: Gigabyte Technology (2376.TW)

### Page 6

Gigabyte Technology (2376.TW)
15 August 2026 Citi Research
6
Gigabyte Technology
Company description
Established in 1986, listed in 1998, and headquartered in Taipei, Taiwan,
Gigabyte is a leading designer and manufacturer of technology hardware
products including motherboards (MB), graphic cards (VGA), servers/AI
servers, as well as PC and peripherals. By shipments, Gigabyte ranks No.2 in
MB and VGA globally; for servers, Gigabyte provides HPC/AI servers to tier 2/3

CSPs and SMEs. Gigabyte’s primary production facilities are located in Taiwan
and China with an extensive distribution network that spans over 100
countries.
Investment strategy
We rate Gigabyte shares as Buy. As a leading motherboard (MB) and graphic
cards (VGA) supplier, Gigabyte has successfully pivoted from legacy business
to servers/AI servers. By leveraging its strong design and manufacturing
capability, as well as early engagement with GPU vendors, we expect

Gigabyte’s server sales to see robust growth and become a primary revenue
and earnings driver for the company. Additionally, the new Nvidia GPU cycle
and multiple AAA game title launches scheduled for 2025/26 should also
help drive upgrade demand for MB/VGA among gamers. We expect rising
server mix and positive momentum in MB/VGA sales to lead to solid earnings
for Gigabyte throughout our forecast period.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$595 is based on c.14x 2027E EPS. Our target multiple

of 14x is slightly higher than the company's average forward PE since 2023,
when the server business started to become a more meaningful driver.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) slower-than-expected growth in AI servers; 2) weaker-than-
expected GPU replacement demand; 3) intense peer competition; and 4)
tariffs and FX volatility.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned

### Page 1

Alchip

Solid Trn3 ramp at improving production 
margins; Trn4 chipset tape out on track

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 6,100 TWD | Price: 4,210 TWD

Resilient 2Q/2H26 GMs even with strong Trainium3 ramp 
2Q26 sales was +83% QoQ with production business outgrowing NRE driven by initial 
contribution of Trainium3 from June along with healthy China ADAS SoC demand. With 
mix skewed to lower margins production, headcount expansion, annual salary hike and 
more EDA usage, 2Q26 GM/OpM was 35%/22% (vs. 50%/33% in 1Q26), taking 2Q26 
EPS to NT$20.02. While its production business could reach 80-90% of its sales in 
2H26, GM/OpM is guided to stay 20-25%/15% (Trainium1 at 10-15%/10%) on its 
growing value add in back-end design and supply chain support even with COT model. 
Trn3/4 locked in; other CSP CPU/ASIC projects in progress 
We expect Trainium3 to generate US$1.3bn sales to Alchip in ’26 (60% of its total sales) 
and could ramp further in ’27 to US$2.2bn (68% of its sales) before Trainium4 on 2nm 
tapes out in 4Q26 and enters production from 4Q27 for the dollar content uplift on 
higher chiplet design complexity and larger reticle size for the package integrating more 
compute and I/O tiles, along with meaningful volume opportunity as Amazon targets to 
adopt the chipset more aggressively in house and sell Trainium-based hardware noted in 
our preview. On top of the US$4bn revenue in ’28 and US$17bn addressable potential 
(ex-HBM) through the lifecycle for Trainium4, Alchip is engaged with other hyperscalers 
potentially on N5 ARM CPUs and certain part of the ASIC projects for contribution in ‘28. 
Firmer ‘26-28 earnings visibility at compelling valuation 
On ramping Trainium3 revenue and better Trainium4 project visibility supplemented by 
better than feared margins profile for the project from 2H26-2027, we lift ‘26/’27 EPS 
from NT$132.78/NT$201.25 to NT$132.94/NT$204.17. We keep Buy rating and raise 
PO to NT$6,100 (vs. NT$6,000 prior) still on 30x/19x ‘27/28e P/E (vs. its 6-53x range). 
Our analysis shows every 100k incremental Trn3/4 chipset units could add its EPS by 
NT$5/NT$15 in ‘27/’28, putting the share 20x/13x ‘27/28e P/E attractive on the cloud 
ASIC TAM upside.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Alchip is a pure IC design service company headquartered in 
Taiwan and has strong presence in Greater China. Its focus 
is on advanced AI/HPC chip-design, largely built on leading 
edge process by the foundry. Beyond Greater China and 
computing chips, it also has customers in US/Europe/Japan, 
etc, and from networking/gaming areas.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Alchip, as we still like its industry 
position for leading edge chip-design, as well as its visibility 
of sustained growth into 2027 along with continuous 
ramps from key projects on mass production side.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
Alchip (ALCPF) 
Our NT$6,100 PO is based on 30x 2027E P/E, at midcycle of the company's historical 
trading range of 6-53x. In our view, this is backed by 1) operating/net margin of 
18%/17% in 2026-27E, 2) EPS CAGR of 55% during 2026-28E, and 3) ROE of 25-30% in 
2026-28E.

Upside risks: 
1) earlier and faster ramp of other non-AWS projects to enter into production stage 
2) looser restriction from the US on China, where Alchip generates better margin with 
strong demand

Downside risks: 
1) intensified competition pressure from existing and emerging peers, 
2) failure or delay to execute node migration and corresponding product roll-out for 
customers, 
3) a slowdown in foundry node migration, which allows a longer time window for peers 
to catch up in advanced chip-design, or makes AI more easily to replace the chip-design 
service provided by the employees/engineers, 
4) lack of or insufficient supply support (foundry, back-end, etc), especially when demand 
is strong from other customers for these supply chain partners.

King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Preliminary Profit in July
Announced
King Slide announced its preliminary profit for July, per TWSE regulation:
• Revenue was NT$6,407mn (+44% MoM/+356% YoY), 33% of our estimate at
NT$19,555mn (+81% QoQ/+348% YoY) and 40% of the consensus at NT
$15,883mn (+47% QoQ/+264% YoY) for 3Q26.
• Pretax income was NT$5,985mn (+321% YoY; pretax margin at 93.4%), 37%
of our pretax income estimate at NT$16,377mn (margin at 83.7%) and 45% of

consensus at NT$13,170mn (margin at 82.9%) for 3Q26.
• Net income was NT$4,787mn (+321% YoY; EPS NT$50.24; NPM 74.7%), 37%
of our estimated net income at NT$12,938mn (EPS NT$135.76; NPM 66.2%)
and 46% of consensus at NT$10,460mn (EPS NT$108.72; NPM 65.9%) for
3Q26.
Our view:
• We see upside to our estimates and consensus for 3Q26 earnings.
• There should be some regular interest income and one-off FX gains during
the month, but even with that, our back-of-the-envelop calculation points to

over 85% of operating margin in July vs. 82.1% in 2Q26. We believe this
should strengthen the market's conviction about the sustainability of its
margins in 2H26.
• We maintain our view that King Slide's competitiveness of the
comprehensive patent portfolio and strong execution is entrenched and
accentuated amid the fast generation migration in AI applications.
• Stay OW with PT of NT$15,000 (30x 2027 P/E)

Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 77% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.4
vs. the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E with an operating

profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 144: 260817_gs_gigabyte

Source PDF: all report pdf/260817_gs_gigabyte.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-17
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

2Q26 beat: Gigabyte’s 2Q26 revenue was up 41% YoY / 37% QoQ to NT$144bn, or 12% 
/ 9% above our estimate / Bloomberg consensus, which we attribute to the AI server 
business expansion and PC demand pull-in amid memory price hike. GM of 9.6% is lower 
than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, mainly on the rising shipments of 
rack-level AI server and rising cost of key components (e.g. memory, CPU, PCB, etc). 
2Q26 opex ratio is better than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, reflecting the 
improving operational efficiency as shipments ramp up. Overall, 2Q26 net income was 
up 117% YoY / 25% QoQ to NT$6.6bn, or 21% higher than our / Bloomberg consensus.

Earnings revision: We factor in Gigabyte’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 result and raise our 
2026-28E net income by 8% / 14% / 13%, mainly on higher revenues. Our 2026-28E 
revenues are revised up by 16% / 22% / 30%, mainly on more AI servers and higher AI 
server racks’ ASP , considering the migration towards next-generation AI server with 
stronger computing power and more complex design. Our GM is lower given the product 
mix toward AI servers. Our OP income raise is smaller than the GP raise given the R&D 
investments to facilitate AI servers technology development. Our NI raise is smaller than 
the OP income raise given the higher interest expense, reflecting the company’s rising 
debt to support the working capital and capex. The company’s operating cash flow is at 
NT$79bn loss in 1H26 (vs. NT$5bn gain in 2025A).

Valuation: We continue to use near-term P/E to derive our 12M TP . Our 2027E target 
P/E is at 14.7x (vs. previously at 16.8x) applied to diluted EPS, still derived from 
correlation of peers’ forward year P/E to avg. EPS YoY (methodology unchanged), 
reflecting the sector de-rating on AI server ODMs amid the model transition and market

concerns on consumer electronics end demand under the high memory costs. With our 
updated target P/E and earnings estimates, our 12M TP is unchanged at NT$487. The 
target P/E of 14.7x is within the company’s historical avg. and avg.+1std forward trading 
P/E. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is 
based on a 14.7x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

34.35 億元 、 持平YoY， 稅後淨利26.6 億元 、+1%YoY，

二、2Q26 年三大產品線同步季增雙位數，其中又以航
太扣件為最，電子產品次之，最後是機構產品 
2Q26 營收比重，電子產品占 54%(其中四成來自國防、
車用占雙位數 、 公共事業占高個位數 ， 餘有警消 、 能源、
零售等)、+36%QoQ、+26%YoY，機構產品占 34%(含
綜合機構產品及汽車機構產品約 1/4)、+11%QoQ、
+1%YoY，航太扣件(豐達科技)占 12%、+14%QoQ、
+40%YoY。三大產品均有雙位數季成長，尤以電子產品
為最，航太扣件次之，機構產品亦有低雙位數成長。 
累計 1H26 年營收比重，電子產品占52%、+14%YoY，
機構產品占 36%、-7%YoY， 航太扣件占12%、+35%YoY，
三大產品線以電子產品及航太扣件年增雙位數為最，餘
機構產品衰退幅度逐步收斂。 
三、2026 年資本支出約 8~12 億元，其中主要以豐達科
擴產為主，餘 3~5 億元，擴產桃園龜山製造基地 
神基 2023 年資本支出達 5-7 億元，2024 年資本支出達
14.6 億元。2025 年資本支出 18 億元，其中神基 9 億多
購買設備及越南廠房，餘 9 億元來自豐達科的馬來西亞
廠及設備等支出。2026 年資本支出預將落在 8~12 億元
左右，其中豐達科資本支出較高，神基 3~5 億元，主要
支出是桃園龜山設立一個製造基地，整修完後擴張強固
型電腦生產製造，Server 機架及電池封裝 BBU 亦有發
展。 
四、展望 2026 年，強固型電腦仍有雙位數成長，PC 相
關綜合機構件年衰退高個位數至低雙位數，汽車機構件
優於預期亦雙位數成長，推估營收年增 11%，稅後 EPS 
9.34 元 
展望 2026 年，整體營運方面，維持2026 年上、下半年
營收比重分別為 48~50%、52~50%的預期 ， 下半年營運
將優於上半年，全年營收成長 5%至 10%的目標可望順
利達成。其中，強固電腦全年營收仍維持成長 20%至
30%的預期，主要動能除既有軍用市場外，公用事業、
運輸及公共安全等新應用也持續拓展。其中，公司已切
入美國大型大眾運輸客戶，涵蓋巴士、火車及校車等應
用，4Q26 初也將發表新一代產品，後續動能看正向； 
與 PC 相關的綜合機構件受到 PC 產業在漲價循環下，
終端承受能力保守看待影響，全年則預估衰退 8%至
12%，目前表現仍落在原先預期區間，但下半年已出現
回溫跡象。不過神基積極拓展高附加價值應用，在碳纖

聯亞(3081.TT) 
產業：電子–通信網路 
CW Laser 矽光產品需求持續強勁，擴充產能因應 CSP 客戶需求 
 投資評價與建議 
聯亞CW Laser需求成⻑與後段切割測試代⼯產能增加 ， 預估2026/2027/2028
年稅後EPS為20.84/48.42/88.72元，考量SiPho技術逐步成熟以及聯亞為亞洲
最大獨立磷化銦磊晶廠，從2028年獲利評價，給予40倍本益比。 
 重點評論分析 
受惠矽光產品出貨增與後段產能開出，2Q26營收成⻑，3Q26營收持續走升：
2Q26營收季增35%至12.2億元，係因矽光產品出貨量增加，受惠產品組合轉
佳外，同時反映轉嫁磷化銦基板漲價予客戶，毛利率上升2.69ppts至57.5%，
稅後EPS為5.06元 ， 除權後股本追溯調整之稅後EPS為4.62元 。 隨矽光產品需求
持續增加以及下游代⼯廠後段切割、測試等產能逐步開出，預估3Q26營收季
增28.4%至15.7億元，稅後EPS為5.75元。 
持續拓展矽光客戶與產能、機台擴充挹注營收動能： 展望2026至2027年，聯
亞預計2026年矽光客產品客戶數量將成⻑至10家以上，其中3.2T以CW Laser
為主之矽光產品預計於2H26量產 。 曝光製程方面 ， 目前主要使用E-beam設備 ，
2026年將擴展另外2種技術：奈米壓印技術與DUV深紫外光微影技術。聯亞目
前奈米壓印技術5年前已經進入量產，使用奈米壓印技術製造之CW Laser已量
產中，2027年新增之機器設備亦會加入量產；DUV深紫外光微影技術主要用
於生產6吋以上之基板 ， DUV月產能可達數萬片 ， 量產時程則視交機時間而定 。  
 投資風險 
後段產能開出進度不如預期。

| 營運概況  
受惠矽光產品出貨增加與後段產能開出，2Q26營收成⻑，3Q26營收持續走升：2Q26營收季增35%至12.2億元，係因
矽光產品出貨量增加，受惠矽光產品出貨增加帶動產品組優化外，同時反映轉嫁磷化銦基板漲價予客戶，毛利率上升
2.69ppts至57.5%，營業利益為5.73億元，營業利益率為46.9%，營業費用較上⼀季增加約700萬元至1.29億元，原因為
隨獲利增加依比例提列之員⼯分紅費用增加，折舊費用約為7,500萬元，較1Q26增加約500萬元，匯兌收益約為439萬
元，稅前淨利5.86億元，稅後淨利為4.68億元，稅後EPS為5.06元，除權後股本追溯調整之稅後EPS為4.62元。隨矽光產
品需求持續增加以及下游代⼯廠產能逐步開出，預估3Q26營收季增28.4%至15.7億元，稅後EPS為5.75元。 
持續拓展矽光客戶與產能、機台擴充挹注營收動能：展望2026至2027年，聯亞預計2026年矽光客產品客戶數量將成⻑
至10家以上，其中3.2T以CW Laser為主之矽光產品預計於2H26量產。資本支出與產能方面，目前累積已公告之資本支
出金額約60億元 ， 主要用於擴充前段MOCVD製程產能以及購置曝光蝕刻製程設備 ， 預估2027年底MOCVD產能較2026
年底增加100%以上，另外2027年之產能將年增150%以上，惟矽光產品供需仍緊俏，公司將持續拓展產能，根據公司
規劃，折舊金額將⻑期佔營收8~12%以下，財務狀況無虞。曝光製程方面，目前主要使用E-beam設備，2026年將擴展
另外2種技術：奈米壓印技術以及DUV深紫外光微影技術。聯亞之奈米壓印技術5年前已經進入量產，目前使用奈米壓印
技術製造之CW Laser皆已在量產中，明年度新增加的機器設備亦會加入量產；DUV深紫外光微影技術主要為生產6吋以
上之基板做準備，由於該設備屬半導體等級且全球僅有⼀家公司供應，故必須提前下單，DUV⼀個月產能可達數萬片之
水準，可支應未來3~5年之量產所需，量產時程則視交機時間而定。綜上所述，預估2026/2027/2028年營收年增
155.6%/120%/73.4%至56.3/123.8/214.7億元，稅後EPS為20.84/48.42/88.72元。

主要競爭對手：

蘇俊嘉 andy.su@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

優群 (3217 TT) 
高價庫存持續去化，NB/PC 出貨不明朗

永豐觀點 
受 NB/PC 出貨衰退影響，高價庫存去化較緩慢，2Q26 毛利率持續呈壓。再次下
修 NB/PC 出貨預估，僅在 Metal Bar 、CAMM 系列及 TypeC 產品可保持較佳成
長動能，股價評價位階低，可逢低長線佈局。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議：公司再次下修 NB/PC 出貨預估，且客戶更改記憶體設計情形
短期 SO-DIMM 需求恐將下滑，相關產品線如SO-DIMM、M.2 及 U-DIMM 今明
年動能皆較不明朗。研究部下修今明年獲利預估，預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 分別為
11.06 及 12.31 元。維持買進投資建議，因獲利調整目標價由 213 元調降至 172
元(14X 2027F EPS)。

### Page 2

公司簡介：優群成立於 1987 年，於 2004 年掛牌上櫃，主要經營業務為電子零組
件及連接器產品之設計、製造及銷售。產品廣泛應用於電子產業，涵蓋 NB、伺服
器、桌上型電腦及手機等領域。終端客戶皆為國際知名大廠，目前主要生產據點包
括台灣新竹、中國昆山、遂寧及越南。目前越南廠一期已於4Q25 投產，而二廠部
分預計將在 2H26 規劃。2Q26 主要各產品比重分別為：SO-DIMM 28% 、M.2 
26%、TypeC 13%、Long-DIMM 11%、Metal Bar 12%、其他 10%。 
高價庫存持續去化 ， 出貨下修營收獲利不如預期 ：2Q26 營收 9.47 億元(-10% YoY，
-0.1% QoQ)，稅後獲利 2.14 億元(+2.9% YoY)，EPS 2.38 元，SO DIMM 及 M.2
產品仍是受到 NB 出貨持續下修影響分別年減 29%及 18%，其中 SO DIMM 衰退
幅度較原先預期嚴重 ；Long-DIMM 部分 ，PC 出貨亦呈現衰退 ， 年減18%；Metal 
Bar 部分，美系品牌手機需求穩健及新 NB 機種採用，成長近 47%。2Q26 單季毛
利率 47.52%，低於原先預估的 49.15%，主要因黃金等高價庫存隨出貨下修本季
去化較慢，毛利率持續受壓抑。 
再次下修 NB/PC 產品出貨，營收動能疲緩：優群今明年各產品線主要動能如下：
(1) SO-DIMM 衰退情況較預期嚴重：目前筆電出貨仍不理想，客戶端持續受限於
記憶體、CPU 缺貨影響，被迫更改設計及調度機種，導致 SO DIMM 需求將持續
下降。研究部預期產品線年衰退幅度恐擴大至20%，明年產品線展望亦尚未明朗。
(2) Long-DIMM 桌機與 Server 用呈現兩極：優群去年有兩家美系 CSP 客戶訂
單，今年預計持續加量，在 R-DIMM 部分出貨符合預期。惟桌機出貨方面，同受
記憶體缺貨影響，U-DIMM 大幅下滑，使整體產品線成長呈壓。目前R-DIMM 出
貨佔比已逾 6-7 成，R-DIMM 出貨佔比提升可改善產品線毛利率 。 下修整體Long-
Dimm 今明年呈小幅成長。(3) M.2 亦受 NB 出貨下修，產品線僅持平至小幅成
長：優群目前 M.2 產品市佔仍逾 50%，高階 NB 逐步導入 PCIe Gen5 SSD、Wifi 
7，規格升級亦使 M.2 相關產品線保有成長動能，惟亦因筆電出貨較不明朗，預期
產品線僅持平至小幅成長看待。目前 Gen5-1A 佔比約 10%，ASP 較高。NB 廠因
高階新機種筆電增加 SSD 插槽，預估 Gen5 滲透率可持續增加。(4) Metal Bar 獨
供美系品牌應用 ，今明年持續保持高成長 ：目前微型沖壓件 Metal Bar 獨家供應美
系品牌手機，應用於 SiP 模組。及新訂單美系品牌 NB 應用在 Wifi Module ，其
ASP 為倍數，預期今年產品線成長可逾 30%。而在 NB 新應用上明年新機預計導
入 Wifi 8，Metal Bar 用量將呈 3 倍提升，明年成長性亦可期。(5) TypeC 穩定成
長，客戶改變新設計 ASP 可以提升：TypeC 多為專案基礎，應用在高階機種如
Thunderbolt 5 客戶採用優群產品 ， 預期今年呈雙位數成長 。 在明年部分 ， 因TypeC
產品客戶改變設計欲使連接器可做替換，預期 ASP 將可提升 50%，預期明年產品
線亦可保持成長 。(6) CAMM/CABB 持續出貨 ， 惟亦受NB 出貨下修影響 ：LPDDR5

3

CAMM 2、CABB 皆已持續出貨，今年產線將擴充增速。2Q26 CABB 客戶庫存調
整影響出貨下降。而今年中系 NB 客戶高階產品導入 CAMM 666 pin ，5 月已量
產出貨產品目前月產能 50K， 仍是些微受到記憶體模組影響 。 略為下修今年營收佔
比可逾 3-5%。在明年部分，中系 NB 客戶目前 CAMM 需求穩定、美系 NB 客戶
亦可在 2Q27 量產，高階機種約占 10%，單價為 SO DIMM 10 倍，對整體產品線
明年展望亦相對樂觀。整體而言，今年 PC/NB 出貨再次下修，展望較不明朗。持
續觀察 NB 更改設計情況是否為短期影響，下一世代 DDR6 產品優群具備競爭利
基，長線成長動能較可期。 
未來營運及獲利預估：評估優群今年整體營收恐較疲軟，3Q26 高價庫存消化完畢
毛利率應可改善。預估 3Q26 營收 11.39 億元(+20% QoQ ，+1%YoY)，毛利率
48.29%，稅後獲利 2.85 億元(+33%QoQ，-25% YoY) ，EPS 3.17 元。調整預估
2026/2027 年營收分別 41.09 及 43.67 億元(-1% YoY ，+6.3% YoY) ，毛利率分
別為 48.34%及 49.67%，稅後獲利分別為 9.95 及 11.08 億元(-13%YoY，+11% 
YoY)，EPS 分別為 11.06 及 12.31 元。維持買進投資建議，下修今明年獲利，目
標價由 213 元調降至 172 元(14X 2027F EPS)。

公司簡介 
臻鼎-KY 為全球最大 FPC 製造廠商，前身
為鴻勝科技 ， 隸屬於鴻海集團 。 公司以軟板
起家 ，1H26 主要產線為行動通訊(53.6%)、

廖貫捷 kuanchieh.liao@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

臻鼎-KY (4958 TT) 
ABF 及 1.6T 光模組帶動，2Q26 毛利率優於預期

永豐觀點 
臻鼎高值化產品需求帶動 2Q26 財報優於預期 ， 在ABF 及 1.6T 光模組帶動下產品
組合轉佳，獲利可望重回高峰水準並延續至 2028 年。 
 投資評價與建議 
臻鼎 2Q26 毛利率 23.1%優於預期 3.5 個百分點，在 1.6T 光模組、ABF 以及 AI 
Server 產品占比由 2025 年的 11%提升至 2028 年的 40%下，營運結構由消費性
電子轉向 AI 領域，上調 2026-2028 年稅後 EPS 至 16.18、23.92 以及 30.78 元，
投資建議維持買進，考量股價先前因資本市場去槓桿修正至合理價位，改以 2027
年獲利評價，目標價調整至 658 元(29X2027 完全稀釋 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
臻鼎-KY 企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面
向平均得分分別為 A+等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

臻鼎-KY 為全球最大 PCB 製造商：臻鼎-KY 為全球最大 FPC 製造廠商，前身為鴻
勝科技，隸屬於鴻海集團。公司以軟板起家，1H26 主要產線為行動通訊(53.6%)、
電腦消費(24.8%)、伺服器/光模塊/IC 載板及其他(21.6%)，主要客戶為 APPLE、
Microsoft、Google、SONY、華為、OPPO、VIVO 等，競爭對手包含 Mektron、
Fujikura、M-Flex、Sumitomo、Nitto Denko 以及 Interflex。 
Server 及載板貢獻，2Q26 財報優於預期：臻鼎 2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.14 元，優於研
究部預估的 1.43 元水準，其中毛利率達 23.1%優於預期 3.5 個百分點主要原因來
自於 AI Server 需求拉動高階 HDI、HLC 以及 ABF 需求，帶動整體產品組合轉佳
並抵銷上游 CCL 漲價壓力，整體而言臻鼎 2Q26 本業表現優於預期。 
旺季加持及 1.6T 光模組出貨，3Q26 營收 QoQ+38.7%：展望 3Q26，除了進入
美系客戶拉貨旺季外，臻鼎 1.6T 光模組板材亦可望同步出貨，由於採用 mSAP 製
程，在供應鏈產能有限下，毛利率優於公司平均30%以上，進一步拉升產品組合。
研究部預估單季營收 671.51 億元 (+38.7%QoQ，+41.8%YoY)，毛利率來到
25.1%，稅後淨利 58.12 億元(+143.0%QoQ，+143.0%YoY)，單季稅後 EPS 5.20
元(原估 5.04 元)。 
高值化產品帶動，2026 年稅後 EPS 上調至 16.18 元：展望 2026 年，隨著高值化
產品陸續發酵，另一方面在 Agentic AI 帶動下 AI 晶片產品需求由 GPU 擴散至
CPU 亦可望帶動 ABF 需求向上，2H26 ABF 報價可望反應供給吃緊逐季上揚 5-
10%， 研究部考量臻鼎高階產品(Server+光模組+ABF)2026 年佔比可望由 21%再
度上調至 23%，且毛利率達 25-35%下帶動產品組合轉佳，預估 2026 年營收
2414.95 億元(+32.3%YoY)， 毛利率24.4%， 稅後淨利180.76 億元(+166.2%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 上調至 16.18 元(原估 15.84 元)。 
Server+ABF+光模組 2030 年占比 45-50%，產品組合持續轉佳：臻鼎 1.6T 光模
組可望於 2H26 出貨，在高速傳輸需求下訊號阻抗降低，PCB 傳統蝕刻法線路粗
糙度將受到檢驗 ， 另一方面在高溫環境中板材熱膨脹後鑽孔對位精度需求亦大幅提
高，因此類載板 mSAP 製程將被大幅採用，由於過去 mSAP 產品僅於 iPhone 主
板中使用，主要供應商為臻鼎-KY(4958 TT)、華通(2313 TT)、欣興(3037 TT)以及
TTM(TTMI US)，整體能穩定大量供應廠商有限，長線而言 1.6T 甚至 3.2T 光模組
以及 Switch 主板對 mSAP 需求亦可望達 iPhone 的 30-35%， 且毛利率高於25%，

頌勝 2Q26  EPS1.70 元； 1H26 累計 EPS3.32 元， YoY+145.9% ， 已 正 式 超 越
2025 年度的 3.24 元 ：  
頌勝 2Q26 營收 5.71 億元， QoQ+2.11% ， YoY+5.76% ， 毛 利 率 52.08% ，較
去 年 呈 現 結 構 性 優 化 。 營 業 費 用2.03 億 元 ， 增 加 主 因 第 二 季 掛 牌 上 市 產 生
之 一 次 性 相 關 費 用 ； 營 業 淨 利9,410 萬 元 ， 淨 利 率16.49% 。2Q26 單季 EPS1.70
元；1H26 累計 EPS3.32 元，YoY+145.9% ，已 正 式 超 越 2025 年 度 的 3.24 元。
半 導 體 材 料 營 收 比 重 攀 升 至 66% ， 帶 動 1H26 毛 利 率 達 53.91% 。  
2H26 出 貨 增 速 再 放 大 ，受 惠 於 AI/HPC 推 動 之 先 進 封 裝 CoWoS 、SoIC 及先
進製程 4nm 推進至 3nm/2nm 強 勁 需 求 ， 公 司 預 期 下 半 年 合 併 營 收 與 毛 利 率
均 將 優 於 上 半 年 ， 半 導 體 出 貨 YoY 增 幅 將 顯 著 超 越 上 半 年 的 25% 。  
對 先 進 封 裝 客 戶 90 天 DOI 備 貨 要 求 與 產 能 吃 緊，短 期 將 關 鍵 瓶 頸 製 程 轉 換
為 24 小 時 四 班 二 輪 制 ； 預 計 4Q26 CMP SoftPad 新 線 量 產 ， 中 國 合 肥 觀 勝
廠 放 量 ， 台 灣 中 科 新 廠 預 計 2Q27 擴 產 啟 動 。 
2023 –2025 年 度 整 體 CAGR 達 25%，2024 -2026 年 半 導 體 業 務 YoY 成 長 率 分
別為 36%、15% 及 25%(1H26) ，隨 著 摩 爾 定 律 推 進 至 3nm/2nm 及 GAA 架 構，
加 上 先 進 封 裝(CoWoS 、SoIC3D 堆 疊、Chiplet 、Hybrid  Bonding) 與 HBM(TSV
矽 穿 孔 )的 全 面 普 及 ， 晶 圓 與 封 裝 結 構 複 雜 度 呈 指 數 級 上 升， 化 學 機 械 研 磨
(CMP) 與 平 坦 化 技 術 已 成 為 決 定 晶 片 良 率 的 最 關 鍵 製 程 耗 材 之 一。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 8 月 17 日

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為 四 個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」 或「區間操作」 之投資評
等，就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基
本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。
